From Wikipedia, the free encyclopedia

Annual award for art direction in film

Academy Award for Best Production Design  
---  
Country| United States  
Presented by| [Academy of Motion Picture Arts and Sciences](/wiki/Academy_of_Motion_Picture_Arts_and_Sciences "Academy of Motion Picture Arts and Sciences") (AMPAS)  
First award| May 16, 1929; 96 years ago (1929-05-16) (for films released during the [1927](/wiki/1927_in_film "1927 in film")/[1928](/wiki/1928_in_film "1928 in film") film season)  
Most recent winner| [Tamara Deverell](/wiki/Tamara_Deverell "Tamara Deverell"), [Shane Vieau](/wiki/Shane_Vieau "Shane Vieau")  
 _[Frankenstein](/wiki/Frankenstein_\(2025_film\) "Frankenstein \(2025 film\)")_ ([2025](/wiki/98th_Academy_Awards "98th Academy Awards"))  
  
The **Academy Award for Best Production Design** recognizes achievement for [art direction](/wiki/Art_direction "Art direction") in [film](/wiki/Film "Film"). The category's original name was **Best Art Direction** , but was changed to its current name in 2012 for the [85th Academy Awards](/wiki/85th_Academy_Awards "85th Academy Awards").[1] This change resulted from the Art Directors' branch of the [Academy of Motion Picture Arts and Sciences](/wiki/Academy_of_Motion_Picture_Arts_and_Sciences "Academy of Motion Picture Arts and Sciences") (AMPAS) being renamed the Designers' branch. Since [1947](/wiki/19th_Academy_Awards "19th Academy Awards"), the award is shared with the set decorators. It is awarded to the best interior design in a film.[2]

The films below are listed with their production year (for example, the [2000 Academy Award](/wiki/72nd_Academy_Awards "72nd Academy Awards") for Best Art Direction is given to a film from 1999). In the lists below, the winner of the award for each year is shown first, followed by the other nominees in alphabetical order. 

## Superlatives

[[edit](/w/index.php?title=Academy_Award_for_Best_Production_Design&action=edit&section=1 "Edit section: Superlatives")]

Category  | Name  | Superlative  | Notes   
---|---|---|---  
Most Awards  | [Cedric Gibbons](/wiki/Cedric_Gibbons "Cedric Gibbons") | 11 awards  | Awards resulted from 39 nominations.[3][4]  
Most Nominations  | 39 nominations  | Nominations resulted in 11 awards.   
Most Nominations   
(without ever winning)  | [Roland Anderson](/wiki/Roland_Anderson "Roland Anderson") | 15 nominations  | Nominations resulted in no awards.[4]  
  
## Winners and nominees

[[edit](/w/index.php?title=Academy_Award_for_Best_Production_Design&action=edit&section=2 "Edit section: Winners and nominees")]

indicates the winner

[](/wiki/File:Cedric_Gibbons_Best_Art_Direction_April_1930.jpg)[Cedric Gibbons](/wiki/Cedric_Gibbons "Cedric Gibbons") holding his first Academy Award for Best Production Design on April 1930. Gibbons designed the Oscar statuette that's still in use each year.

### 1950s

[[edit](/w/index.php?title=Academy_Award_for_Best_Production_Design&action=edit&section=6 "Edit section: 1950s")]

## Notes

[[edit](/w/index.php?title=Academy_Award_for_Best_Production_Design&action=edit&section=14 "Edit section: Notes")]

  1. **^** The 2nd Academy Awards is unique in being the only occasion where there were no official nominees. Subsequent research by AMPAS has resulted in a list of unofficial or de facto nominees, based on records of which films were evaluated by the judges.
  2. **^** Prior to 1941, only credited art directors and assistant art directors were eligible for nomination.
  3. **^** [Republic Pictures](/wiki/Republic_Pictures "Republic Pictures") submitted _[Sis Hopkins](/wiki/Sis_Hopkins_\(1941_film\) "Sis Hopkins \(1941 film\)")_ and it was initially named as a nominee. However, the studio later withdrew the film from consideration and it is not considered an official nominee.
  4. **^** [United Artists](/wiki/United_Artists "United Artists") submitted _[Song of the Open Road](/wiki/Song_of_the_Open_Road "Song of the Open Road")_ and it was initially named as a nominee. However, the studio later withdrew the film from consideration and it is not considered an official nominee.
  5. ^ _**a**_ _**b**_ In 1957 and 1958, black-and-white and color films competed in a combined Best Art Direction category.



## Shortlisted finalists

[[edit](/w/index.php?title=Academy_Award_for_Best_Production_Design&action=edit&section=15 "Edit section: Shortlisted finalists")]

Finalists for Best Production Design were selected by branch members, who voted for ten finalists which were screened to determine the five nominees.[32]

Year  | Finalists  | Ref   
---|---|---  
1967  | _[Barefoot in the Park](/wiki/Barefoot_in_the_Park_\(film\) "Barefoot in the Park \(film\)")_ , _[Bonnie and Clyde](/wiki/Bonnie_and_Clyde_\(film\) "Bonnie and Clyde \(film\)")_ , _[The Flim-Flam Man](/wiki/The_Flim-Flam_Man "The Flim-Flam Man")_ , _[The Happiest Millionaire](/wiki/The_Happiest_Millionaire "The Happiest Millionaire")_ , _[In Like Flint](/wiki/In_Like_Flint "In Like Flint")_ | [33]  
1968  | _[Funny Girl](/wiki/Funny_Girl_\(film\) "Funny Girl \(film\)")_ , _[The Killing of Sister George](/wiki/The_Killing_of_Sister_George_\(film\) "The Killing of Sister George \(film\)")_ , _[Never a Dull Moment](/wiki/Never_a_Dull_Moment_\(1968_film\) "Never a Dull Moment \(1968 film\)")_ , _[The Odd Couple](/wiki/The_Odd_Couple_\(film\) "The Odd Couple \(film\)")_ , _[Planet of the Apes](/wiki/Planet_of_the_Apes_\(1968_film\) "Planet of the Apes \(1968 film\)")_ | [34]

Academy Award for Best Production Design  
---  
Interior Decoration   
(1927–1939)| 

  * 1927/1928: [William Cameron Menzies](/wiki/William_Cameron_Menzies "William Cameron Menzies")
  * 1928/1929: [Cedric Gibbons](/wiki/Cedric_Gibbons "Cedric Gibbons")
  * 1929/1930: [Herman Rosse](/wiki/Herman_Rosse "Herman Rosse")
  * 1930/1931: [Max Rée](/wiki/Max_R%C3%A9e "Max Rée")
  * 1931/1932: [Gordon Wiles](/wiki/Gordon_Wiles "Gordon Wiles")
  * 1932/1933: [William S. Darling](/wiki/William_S._Darling "William S. Darling")
  * 1934: [Cedric Gibbons](/wiki/Cedric_Gibbons "Cedric Gibbons"), [Fredric Hope](/wiki/Fredric_Hope "Fredric Hope")
  * 1935: [Richard Day](/wiki/Richard_Day_\(art_director\) "Richard Day \(art director\)")
  * 1936: [Richard Day](/wiki/Richard_Day_\(art_director\) "Richard Day \(art director\)")
  * 1937: [Stephen Goosson](/wiki/Stephen_Goosson "Stephen Goosson")
  * 1938: [Carl Jules Weyl](/wiki/Carl_Jules_Weyl "Carl Jules Weyl")
  * 1939: [Lyle R. Wheeler](/wiki/Lyle_R._Wheeler "Lyle R. Wheeler")

  
Black & White / Color   
(1940–1946)|

Willis](/wiki/Edwin_B._Willis "Edwin B. Willis")

Wright](/wiki/Joseph_C._Wright "Joseph C. Wright"), and [Thomas Little](/wiki/Thomas_Little "Thomas Little")
  * 1943 (bw): [James Basevi](/wiki/James_Basevi "James Basevi"), [William S. Darling](/wiki/William_S._Darling "William S. Willis](/wiki/Edwin_B._Willis "Edwin B. Willis") / (c): [Wiard Ihnen](/wiki/Wiard_Ihnen "Wiard Ihnen") and [Thomas Little](/wiki/Thomas_Little "Thomas Little")

Hughes") / (c): [Cedric Gibbons](/wiki/Cedric_Gibbons "Cedric Gibbons"), [Paul Groesse](/wiki/Paul_Groesse "Paul Groesse"), and [Edwin B. Willis](/wiki/Edwin_B._Willis "Edwin B. Willis")

1981–2000| 

  * 1981: [Norman Reynolds](/wiki/Norman_Reynolds "Norman Reynolds") and [Leslie Dilley](/wiki/Leslie_Dilley "Leslie Dilley") (art); [Michael D. Ford](/wiki/Michael_D._Ford "Michael D. Ford") (set)
  * 1982: [Stuart Craig](/wiki/Stuart_Craig "Stuart Craig") and [Robert W. Laing](/wiki/Robert_W._Laing "Robert W. Laing") (art); [Michael Seirton](/wiki/Michael_Seirton "Michael Seirton") (set)
  * 1983: [Anna Asp](/wiki/Anna_Asp "Anna Asp") (art)
  * 1984: [Patrizia von Brandenstein](/wiki/Patrizia_von_Brandenstein "Patrizia von Brandenstein") (art); [Karel Černý](/wiki/Karel_%C4%8Cern%C3%BD_\(art_director\) "Karel Černý \(art director\)") (set)
  * 1985: [Stephen B. Grimes](/wiki/Stephen_B._Grimes "Stephen B. Grimes") (art); [Josie MacAvin](/wiki/Josie_MacAvin "Josie MacAvin") (set)
  * 1986: [Gianni Quaranta](/wiki/Gianni_Quaranta "Gianni Quaranta") and [Brian Ackland-Snow](/wiki/Brian_Ackland-Snow "Brian Ackland-Snow") (art); [Brian Savegar](/wiki/Brian_Savegar "Brian Savegar") and [Elio Altramura](/wiki/Elio_Altramura "Elio Altramura") (set)
  * 1987: [Ferdinando Scarfiotti](/wiki/Ferdinando_Scarfiotti "Ferdinando Scarfiotti") (art); [Bruno Cesari](/wiki/Bruno_Cesari "Bruno Cesari") and [Osvaldo Desideri](/wiki/Osvaldo_Desideri "Osvaldo Desideri") (set)
  * 1988: [Stuart Craig](/wiki/Stuart_Craig "Stuart Craig") (art); [Gérard James](/wiki/G%C3%A9rard_James "Gérard James") (set)
  * 1989: [Anton Furst](/wiki/Anton_Furst "Anton Furst") (art); [Peter Young](/wiki/Peter_Young_\(set_decorator\) "Peter Young \(set decorator\)") (set)
  * 1990: [Richard Sylbert](/wiki/Richard_Sylbert "Richard Sylbert") (art); [Rick Simpson](/wiki/Rick_Simpson "Rick Simpson") (set)
  * 1991: [Dennis Gassner](/wiki/Dennis_Gassner "Dennis Gassner") (art); [Nancy Haigh](/wiki/Nancy_Haigh "Nancy Haigh") (set)
  * 1992: [Luciana Arrighi](/wiki/Luciana_Arrighi "Luciana Arrighi") (art); [Ian Whittaker](/wiki/Ian_Whittaker "Ian Whittaker") (set)
  * 1993: [Allan Starski](/wiki/Allan_Starski "Allan Starski") (art); [Ewa Braun](/wiki/Ewa_Braun "Ewa Braun") (set)
  * 1994: [Ken Adam](/wiki/Ken_Adam "Ken Adam") (art); [Carolyn Scott](/wiki/Carolyn_Scott "Carolyn Scott") (set)
  * 1995: [Eugenio Zanetti](/wiki/Eugenio_Zanetti "Eugenio Zanetti") (art)
  * 1996: [Stuart Craig](/wiki/Stuart_Craig "Stuart Craig") (art); [Stephenie McMillan](/wiki/Stephenie_McMillan "Stephenie McMillan") (set)
  * 1997: [Peter Lamont](/wiki/Peter_Lamont "Peter Lamont") (art); [Michael D. Ford](/wiki/Michael_D._Ford "Michael D. Ford") (set)
  * 1998: [Martin Childs](/wiki/Martin_Childs "Martin Childs") (art); [Jill Quertier](/wiki/Jill_Quertier "Jill Quertier") (set)
  * 1999: [Rick Heinrichs](/wiki/Rick_Heinrichs "Rick Heinrichs") (art); [Peter Young](/wiki/Peter_Young_\(set_decorator\) "Peter Young \(set decorator\)") (set)
  * 2000: [Timmy Yip](/wiki/Timmy_Yip "Timmy Yip") (art)

  
2001–present| 

  * 2001: [Catherine Martin](/wiki/Catherine_Martin_\(designer\) "Catherine Martin \(designer\)") (art); [Brigitte Broch](/wiki/Brigitte_Broch "Brigitte Broch") (set)
  * 2002: [John Myhre](/wiki/John_Myhre "John Myhre") (art); [Gordon Sim](/wiki/Gordon_Sim "Gordon Sim") (set)
  * 2003: [Grant Major](/wiki/Grant_Major "Grant Major") (art); [Dan Hennah](/wiki/Dan_Hennah "Dan Hennah") and [Alan Lee](/wiki/Alan_Lee_\(illustrator\) "Alan Lee \(illustrator\)") (set)
  * 2004: [Dante Ferretti](/wiki/Dante_Ferretti "Dante Ferretti") (art); [Francesca Lo Schiavo](/wiki/Francesca_Lo_Schiavo "Francesca Lo Schiavo") (set)
  * 2005: [John Myhre](/wiki/John_Myhre "John Myhre") (art); [Gretchen Rau](/wiki/Gretchen_Rau "Gretchen Rau") (set)
  * 2006: [Eugenio Caballero](/wiki/Eugenio_Caballero "Eugenio Caballero") (art); [Pilar Revuelta](/wiki/Pilar_Revuelta "Pilar Revuelta") (set)
  * 2007: [Dante Ferretti](/wiki/Dante_Ferretti "Dante Ferretti") (art); [Francesca Lo Schiavo](/wiki/Francesca_Lo_Schiavo "Francesca Lo Schiavo") (set)
  * 2008: [Donald Graham Burt](/wiki/Donald_Graham_Burt "Donald Graham Burt") (art); [Victor J. Zolfo](/wiki/Victor_J._Zolfo "Victor J. Zolfo") (set)
  * 2009: [Rick Carter](/wiki/Rick_Carter "Rick Carter") and [Robert Stromberg](/wiki/Robert_Stromberg "Robert Stromberg") (art); [Kim Sinclair](/wiki/Kim_Sinclair "Kim Sinclair") (set)
  * 2010: [Robert Stromberg](/wiki/Robert_Stromberg "Robert Stromberg") (art); [Karen O'Hara](/wiki/Karen_O%27Hara "Karen O'Hara") (set)
  * 2011: [Dante Ferretti](/wiki/Dante_Ferretti "Dante Ferretti") (art); [Francesca Lo Schiavo](/wiki/Francesca_Lo_Schiavo "Francesca Lo Schiavo") (set)
  * 2012: [Rick Carter](/wiki/Rick_Carter "Rick Carter") (art); [Jim Erickson](/wiki/Jim_Erickson "Jim Erickson") (set)
  * 2013: [Catherine Martin](/wiki/Catherine_Martin_\(designer\) "Catherine Martin \(designer\)") (art); [Beverley Dunn](/wiki/Beverley_Dunn_\(set_decorator\) "Beverley Dunn \(set decorator\)") (set)
  * 2014: [Adam Stockhausen](/wiki/Adam_Stockhausen "Adam Stockhausen") (art); [Anna Pinnock](/wiki/Anna_Pinnock "Anna Pinnock") (set)
  * 2015: [Colin Gibson](/wiki/Colin_Gibson_\(production_designer\) "Colin Gibson \(production designer\)") (art); [Lisa Thompson](/wiki/Lisa_Thompson_\(set_decorator\) "Lisa Thompson \(set decorator\)") (set)
  * 2016: [David Wasco](/wiki/David_and_Sandy_Reynolds-Wasco "David and Sandy Reynolds-Wasco") (art); [Sandy Reynolds-Wasco](/wiki/David_and_Sandy_Reynolds-Wasco "David and Sandy Reynolds-Wasco") (set)
  * 2017: [Paul Denham Austerberry](/wiki/Paul_Denham_Austerberry "Paul Denham Austerberry") (art); [Shane Vieau](/wiki/Shane_Vieau "Shane Vieau") and [Jeff Melvin](/wiki/Jeff_Melvin "Jeff Melvin") (set)
  * 2018: [Hannah Beachler](/wiki/Hannah_Beachler "Hannah Beachler") (art); [Jay Hart](/wiki/Jay_Hart_\(set_decorator\) "Jay Hart \(set decorator\)") (set)
  * 2019: [Barbara Ling](/wiki/Barbara_Ling "Barbara Ling") (art); [Nancy Haigh](/wiki/Nancy_Haigh "Nancy Haigh") (set)
  * 2020: [Donald Graham Burt](/wiki/Donald_Graham_Burt "Donald Graham Burt") (art); [Jan Pascale](/wiki/Jan_Pascale "Jan Pascale") (set)
  * 2021: [Patrice Vermette](/wiki/Patrice_Vermette "Patrice Vermette") (art) and [Zsuzsanna Sipos](/wiki/Zsuzsanna_Sipos "Zsuzsanna Sipos") (set)
  * 2022: [Christian M. Goldbeck](/wiki/Christian_M._Goldbeck "Christian M. Goldbeck") (art) and [Ernestine Hipper](/wiki/Ernestine_Hipper "Ernestine Hipper") (set)
  * 2023: [James Price](/wiki/James_Price_\(art_director\) "James Price \(art director\)") and [Shona Heath](/wiki/Shona_Heath "Shona Heath") (art); [Zsuzsa Mihalek](/wiki/Zsuzsa_Mihalek "Zsuzsa Mihalek") (set)
  * 2024: [Nathan Crowley](/wiki/Nathan_Crowley "Nathan Crowley") (art); [Lee Sandales](/wiki/Lee_Sandales "Lee Sandales") (set)
  * 2025: [Tamara Deverell](/wiki/Tamara_Deverell "Tamara Deverell") (art); [Shane Vieau](/wiki/Shane_Vieau "Shane Vieau") (set)
